<h2 id="assemble-apis-instead-of-copying-algorithms">Assemble APIs instead of copying algorithms</h2>
<p><code>run_capstone</code> starts by parsing the checked corpus and split. It
learns BPE from the training partition, encodes each document without joining
boundaries, fits the alpha-one bigram from the same training tokens, and builds
separate causal epochs for training updates and validation. It does not build
the test epoch at this stage.</p>
<p>Two decoder runs receive the same initialization and batch-order seed. Each
executes all <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>32</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">32</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">32</span></span></span></span> updates. The implementation compares every training event,
loss checkpoint, optimizer moment, selected step, and model value by bit pattern.
Only after replay agrees and validation fixes the selection does the run prepare
final evaluation. <code>TrainingResult</code> owns both the retained selected state and an
independent decoder with the same values. <code>SelectedDecoder</code> borrows both. The run
then materializes the test epoch, records two facts—<span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>436</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">436</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">436</span></span></span></span> windows
and <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">4</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">4</span></span></span></span> mini-batches—and moves the epoch into <code>FinalEvaluator</code>. The evaluator
owns the epoch and the local permission to evaluate it once. Immediately before
opening that local test gate, it verifies that the decoder’s configuration,
ordered names and shapes, and every parameter bit still match the retained
state; this prevents a changed decoder from being evaluated under stale
selection metadata without making another model copy. The evaluator then scores
the borrowed model and consumes its permission. It compares both models on the
same ordered <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>1744</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1744</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">1744</span></span></span></span> overlapping window-target slots, including the same repeated
transition occurrences. The decoder has context capacity four, but its slot
predictions use actual in-window context lengths <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span>, <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">2</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">2</span></span></span></span>, <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">3</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">3</span></span></span></span>, and <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">4</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">4</span></span></span></span>. The
separate policy would score all <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>442</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">442</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">442</span></span></span></span> occurrences once each, use the longest
available causal prefix capped at four tokens and only its newest-position distribution,
and report no numeric mean NLL or PPL.</p>
<p>The permission, not either borrowed object, is the one-use resource. This is an
executable boundary inside this run, not a claim that the repository globally
prevents other code from reading the corpus. Later report assembly retains the
derived counts and metric evidence, not a cloned epoch.</p>
<p>The pipeline retains the lower decoder window-slot mean NLL as an explicitly named
fixed-fixture regression condition; <code>FinalEvaluator</code> itself remains neutral to
which model is lower. The learner evidence uses
<code>decoder_lower_on_fixture:true</code> and records
<code>scope:fixed-fixture-regression</code>, <code>within_run_selection_isolated:true</code>,
<code>independent_generalization_estimate:false</code>, and
<code>architecture_superiority_evidence:false</code>.</p>
<p>The report derives the displayed count of <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>1188</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1188</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">1188</span></span></span></span> learned scalars by calling
<code>primary.selected_state().scalar_count()</code>. That method computes the count by
summing the lengths of the parameter tensors in the retained graph-free state
selected by validation; it neither reads a stored count nor initializes another
decoder.</p>
<p>Final evaluation and durable checkpoint storage have different ownership needs.
Evaluation only borrows the retained selected state and matching decoder. After
evaluation, the checkpoint must coexist with the complete training result, so
<code>Checkpoint::from_snapshot</code> deliberately copies the selected graph-free state
and optimizer persistence state. That copy is required because the training
result and checkpoint must remain independently usable.</p>
<p>Re-encoding the loaded checkpoint must reproduce the saved bytes, and its model
and optimizer bits, BPE ranks, step, and RNG state must all agree. Before
consuming the checkpoint, the pipeline records the tokenizer, selected step,
optimizer state, and saved RNG value needed by later evidence. It then calls
<code>loaded.into_model()</code>, which moves the owned model buffers into a decoder. The
separate <code>At</code> probe compares that decoder with
<code>primary.selected_model()</code> and must match bit for bit. Only then does the loaded
model run cached and complete-prefix generation from prompt <code>A</code> and
the saved RNG state. <code>generation_evidence</code> encodes the prompt once into one
<code>prompt_ids</code> vector. Both generation paths temporarily read that vector through
immutable references. After both calls finish and the local vector is no longer
needed for calculation, <code>GenerationEvidence</code> takes ownership of the same vector;
moving the <code>Vec</code> transfers its existing buffer instead of cloning the prompt IDs
for the report.</p>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFB757">        PipelineStage</span><span style="color:#FF9492">::</span><span style="color:#FFB757">Checkpoint</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        checkpoint</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">save_atomic</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(checkpoint_path</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">as_ref</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    )</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> loaded </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF"> map</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFB757">        PipelineStage</span><span style="color:#FF9492">::</span><span style="color:#FFB757">Checkpoint</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFB757">        Checkpoint</span><span style="color:#FF9492">::</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">load</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(checkpoint_path</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">as_ref</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    )</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> loaded_encoded </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF"> map</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FFB757">PipelineStage</span><span style="color:#FF9492">::</span><span style="color:#FFB757">Checkpoint</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, loaded</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">encode</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">())</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> bytes_roundtrip </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> encoded_checkpoint</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">bytes</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">() </span><span style="color:#FF9492">==</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> loaded_encoded</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">bytes</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">    require</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        bytes_roundtrip,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#ADDCFF">        "loaded checkpoint bytes differ from the saved record"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    )</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        .</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">ok_or_else</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">||</span><span style="color:#FFB757"> PipelineError</span><span style="color:#FF9492">::</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">invariant</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#ADDCFF">"checkpoint lost its byte BPE tokenizer"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">))</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> loaded_rng_state </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> loaded</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">sampling_rng_state</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> loaded_model </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF"> map</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FFB757">PipelineStage</span><span style="color:#FF9492">::</span><span style="color:#FFB757">Checkpoint</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, loaded</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">into_model</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">())</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> logit_probe_text </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#ADDCFF"> "At"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> logit_probe_ids </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> data</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">tokenizer</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">encode_utf8</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(logit_probe_text);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> prompt_logits_bitwise </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF"> logits_bits</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(primary</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">selected_model</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(), </span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">logit_probe_ids)</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        ==</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF"> logits_bits</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">loaded_model, </span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">logit_probe_ids)</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> tokenizer_exact </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> loaded_tokenizer </span><span style="color:#FF9492">==</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> data</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">tokenizer;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">    require</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        model_bits_exact </span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> optimizer_bits_exact </span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> prompt_logits_bitwise </span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> tokenizer_exact,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#ADDCFF">        "checkpoint reload changed model, optimizer, tokenizer, or probe logits"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    )</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> generation </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">        generation_evidence</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">loaded_model, </span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">loaded_tokenizer, loaded_rng_state, config)</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> checkpoints </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> primary</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        .</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">checkpoints</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        .</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">iter</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        .</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">map</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">|</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">checkpoint</span><span style="color:#FF9492">|</span><span style="color:#FFB757"> TrainingCheckpointEvidence</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">            step</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> checkpoint</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">step</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">            train_loss</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> checkpoint</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">train</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">mean_loss</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">            validation_loss</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> checkpoint</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">validation</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">mean_loss</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">            selected</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> checkpoint</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">selected</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        .</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">collect</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> training </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#FFB757"> TrainingEvidence</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        model_config</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> prepared</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">model_config,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        parameter_count</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> primary</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">selected_state</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">scalar_count</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        window_counts</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> [</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">            prepared</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">train</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">window_count</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">            prepared</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">validation</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">window_count</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">            test_window_count,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        ],</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">            prepared</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">train</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">batch_count</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">            prepared</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">validation</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">batch_count</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">            test_batch_count,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        ],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        checkpoints,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        selected_step,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        selected_validation_loss</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> primary</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">selected_validation_loss</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        optimizer_step</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> primary</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">final_optimizer</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">step_count</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        replay_bitwise,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    };</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        bytes</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> encoded_checkpoint</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">bytes</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">len</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        header_bytes</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> encoded_checkpoint</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">header_bytes</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        checksum</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> encoded_checkpoint</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">checksum_label</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        tensor_records</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> encoded_checkpoint</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">tensors</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">len</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        selected_step</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> loaded_selected_step,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        optimizer_step</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> loaded_optimizer_step,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        sampling_rng_state</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> loaded_rng_state,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        bytes_roundtrip,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        model_bits_exact,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        optimizer_bits_exact,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        tokenizer_exact,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        logit_probe_text</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> logit_probe_text</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">to_owned</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        logit_probe_ids,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        prompt_logits_bitwise,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFB757">    Ok</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FFB757">CapstoneRun</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        partitions</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> data</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">partitions,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        tokenizer</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> data</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">tokenizer_evidence,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        training,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        final_evaluation,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        checkpoint</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> checkpoint_evidence,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        generation,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre> </figure>
<p>The runnable evidence requires every final claim together. It rejects a changed
test ordering, a changed lower-decoder slot-metric regression condition, a
replay with different bits, altered checkpoint state or probe logits, or any
cached sampling divergence. That requirement protects known fixture behavior; it
does not promote the ordering into independent generalization evidence.</p>

executable prints this exact report:</p>

<h2 id="follow-the-one-way-pipeline">Follow the one-way pipeline</h2>
<p>The figure begins with fixed document roles and training-only tokenizer learning.
It then follows causal windows into the decoder and places the validation-selected
state before a double-marked local test gate. Test loss has no path back to an
update or selection in this run. The test card identifies the <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>1744</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1744</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">1744</span></span></span></span> observations
as overlapping window-target slots and labels the values as mean NLL in nats per
slot. The separate policy would score all <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>442</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">442</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">442</span></span></span></span> within-document transition
occurrences once each, use the longest causal prefix capped at four tokens and only its
newest-position distribution, and report no numeric mean NLL or PPL. The known
ordering remains a fixed-fixture regression condition, not independent
generalization evidence.</p>
<p>The last two stages ask different equality questions. Checkpoint reload preserves
saved bytes plus model, optimizer, tokenizer, step, and RNG state exactly; logits
for probe <code>At</code> agree bit for bit. Cached generation from prompt
<code>A</code> preserves the complete-prefix token decisions and RNG state.
Before the three token choices, the retained prefixes have lengths <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span>, <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">2</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">2</span></span></span></span>,
and <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">3</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">3</span></span></span></span> tokens. Cache prefill processes the one-token prompt to produce logits
for the first choice; two one-token decode calls then process the first two
generated tokens to produce logits for the next two choices. The cached path’s
instrumented attention work contains <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1+2+3=6</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7278em;vertical-align:-0.0833em"></span><span class="mord">1</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7278em;vertical-align:-0.0833em"></span><span class="mord">2</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">3</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">6</span></span></span></span> score cells; the same prefix
schedule gives the calculated complete-prefix reference count
<span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>14</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1^2+2^2+3^2=14</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.8974em;vertical-align:-0.0833em"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord">1</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8141em"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">2</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.8974em;vertical-align:-0.0833em"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord">2</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8141em"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">2</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.8141em"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord">3</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8141em"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">2</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">14</span></span></span></span>.</p>
<figure class="course-diagram end-to-end-llm-diagram" data-diagram-style="course-v1" data-visualization-id="end-to-end-llm" tabindex="0" aria-labelledby="end-to-end-llm-diagram-title" aria-describedby="end-to-end-llm-diagram-description" data-astro-cid-d53c2kif> <figcaption class="course-diagram__caption" data-astro-cid-d53c2kif> <h3 id="end-to-end-llm-diagram-title" data-astro-cid-d53c2kif>Keep execution one-way and label fixture evidence</h3> <p id="end-to-end-llm-diagram-description" class="course-diagram__description" data-astro-cid-d53c2kif>Follow frozen Rust evidence through training-only BPE, selection, and a locally isolated comparison over 1,744 overlapping window-target slots. A separate unreported metric would score 442 within-document transition occurrences once each with the longest available causal prefix capped at four tokens and only its newest-position distribution; its numeric mean NLL and PPL are not reported. Then follow exact reload and cached generation.</p> </figcaption> <section aria-labelledby="end-to-end-pipeline-title" data-astro-cid-d53c2kif> <h4 id="end-to-end-pipeline-title" data-astro-cid-d53c2kif>Follow the complete program in order</h4> <div class="course-diagram__card-stack" data-astro-cid-d53c2kif> <p class="course-diagram__description" data-astro-cid-d53c2kif>Numbers give executable order. The test stage reports the equal-slot comparison. It also marks the separate 442-transition policy—longest causal prefix capped at four tokens, newest-position distribution only—and states that its numeric mean NLL and PPL are not reported. Double borders mark training-only input, validation selection, locally isolated fixed-fixture evaluation, and exact replay boundaries.</p> <ol class="pipeline course-diagram__grid" <h2 id="predict-before-checking-the-final-trace">Predict before checking the final trace</h2>
<ol>
<li>Which partition teaches the eight BPE merges?</li>
<li>How many decoder parameters receive updates?</li>
<li>What evidence chooses step <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>32</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">32</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">32</span></span></span></span>?</li>
<li>May the test loss change that choice?</li>
<li>How do <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>436</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">436</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">436</span></span></span></span> overlapping test windows become <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>1744</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1744</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">1744</span></span></span></span> window-target slots, and
why are there only <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>442</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">442</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">442</span></span></span></span> within-document transition occurrences?</li>
<li>What is the measured slot mean-NLL gap, how is window-slot perplexity related
to each model’s slot mean NLL, and what evidence scope does the ordering have?</li>
<li>Which checkpoint facts are exact, and which claim belongs only to probe <code>At</code>?</li>
<li>Which literal token IDs follow prompt <code>A</code>?</li>
<li>Why is decoded <code>т␠␠</code> not evidence of translation quality?</li>
<li>What does the second seed-<span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>39</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">39</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">39</span></span></span></span> training run prove?</li>
</ol>
<p>Misconception: <code>access:1</code> means the repository has used this test result only
once. The count belongs to one evaluator instance inside one execution. This run
freezes the state first and materializes test batches afterward, so test cannot
affect that selected state. Later executions reuse the known ordering for
regression checking; determinism makes that repeated comparison reproducible,
not independent.</p>
<details><summary>Check the ten predictions</summary><ol>
<li>Only the <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">8</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">8</span></span></span></span> training documents contribute BPE pair counts.</li>

<span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>5.628342353</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">5.628342353</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">5.628342353</span></span></span></span>.</li>
<li>No. Final test evidence arrives after selection and performs no update.</li>
<li>Each of the <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>436</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">436</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">436</span></span></span></span> stride-one windows has <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">C=4</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0715em">C</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">4</span></span></span></span> target slots, so
<span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>436</mn><mo>⋅</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1744</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">436\cdot4=1744</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">436</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em"></span><span class="mbin">⋅</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">4</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">1744</span></span></span></span>. Overlap repeats a within-document transition occurrence in
as many as four slots. The two separate test documents contain
<span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>444</mn><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>442</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">444-2=442</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7278em;vertical-align:-0.0833em"></span><span class="mord">444</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">2</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">442</span></span></span></span> such occurrences. The separate policy would score each once, use
the longest available causal prefix capped at four tokens and only its newest-position
distribution, and report no numeric mean NLL or PPL.</li>
<li>The reported values are mean NLL in nats per slot, and
<span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>3.981342714</mn><mo>−</mo><mn>3.866087547</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0.115255167</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">3.981342714-3.866087547=0.115255167</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7278em;vertical-align:-0.0833em"></span><span class="mord">3.981342714</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">3.866087547</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">0.115255167</span></span></span></span>. Window-slot perplexity is
<span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>exp</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><msub><mi>ℒ</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></msub><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\exp(\mathcal L_{\mathrm{slot}})</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em"></span><span class="mop">exp</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathcal">L</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.3361em"><span style="top:-2.55em;margin-left:0em;margin-right:0.05em"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathrm mtight">slot</span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.15em"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>, not either mean-NLL value itself. The
lower decoder slot mean-NLL ordering retained by later executions is regression evidence, not an
untouched independent estimate of generalization or architecture superiority.</li>
<li>Re-encoded bytes, model and optimizer bits, BPE ranks, selected step, and RNG
state are exact; only logits for the explicit <code>At</code> probe are compared.</li>
<li>The literal generated IDs are <code>[260,34,34]</code>.</li>
<li>It is one seeded sample from a tiny bilingual corpus, not a semantic benchmark.</li>
<li>It checks that this executable fixture reproduces every recorded training,
validation, optimizer, and model bit under the frozen inputs and toolchain.</li>
</ol></details>

<h2 id="continue-from-the-scalar-reference-not-a-laptop-scale-result">Continue from the scalar reference, not a laptop-scale result</h2>
<p>The scalar reference components now participate in one bounded CPU program: frozen
bilingual data becomes BPE tokens and causal batches; validation selects the
decoder before the local final evaluator receives test batches; both models
score the same ordered overlapping window-target slots; the separate policy
would score <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>442</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">442</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">442</span></span></span></span> within-document transitions once each with the longest causal
prefix capped at four tokens and only its newest-position distribution, but reports no
numeric mean NLL or PPL; the known lower decoder slot mean-NLL ordering
remains only fixed-fixture regression evidence;
checkpoint bytes and state round-trip exactly; the separate <code>At</code> probe reproduces
logits bit for bit; and cached generation from <code>A</code> returns decoded
text. The reference uses explicit scalar CPU loops and <code>f64</code> values. Its
end-to-end result is an integration demonstration, not evidence of useful
language quality or laptop-scale training and inference.</p>
<p>Chapter 40 begins the separately planned extension by recording the reference’s
configuration, evidence, and limits. Later chapters develop and measure the
laptop path; this chapter does not claim those capabilities are already present.
Changing context, model width, tokenizer budget, or sampling policy also changes
which data, mathematical, training, evaluation, persistence, and inference
evidence must be re-established.</p> </div> <nav class="chapter-navigation" aria-label="Chapter navigation" data-chapter-navigation data-astro-cid-44eis63d> <a class="chapter-navigation__chapter
